The pipeline now runs several copies of this application side by side so verify can spread the acceptance suite across them (pipeline decision 0090; see the stack profile's Layout section). Each copy is its own compose project and must publish on its own host ports. Make app/compose/compose.yaml read every published host port, and every address that names one, from a variable defaulting to today's value, so that with no variable set nothing changes: the frontend's published port as ${SDLC_APP_PORT:-4300}; the identity provider's as ${SDLC_IDP_PORT:-8080}; the mail front's as ${SDLC_MAIL_PORT:-8025}; OIDC_ISSUER and KC_HOSTNAME as http://localhost:${SDLC_IDP_PORT:-8080}...; SERVICE_ORIGIN as http://localhost:${SDLC_APP_PORT:-4300}. The identity provider's realm (app/compose/idp) must allow redirects and post-logout redirects to http://localhost:${SDLC_APP_PORT:-4300}, rendered from that variable by the realm renderer the way the password already is.
